 115→\n   116→impl Dispatcher {\n   117→    /// Create a new dispatcher with a configurable ring buffer size.\n   118→    pub fn new(channel_size: usize) -> Self {\n   119→        let (sender, _rx) = broadcast::channel(channel_size);\n   120→        Self { sender }\n   121→    }\n   122→\n   123→    /// Publish an envelope to all subscribers.\n   124→    pub async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) {\n   125→        // broadcast::Sender::send is synchronous but inexpensive.\n   126→        // Failure only occurs when there are no active receivers;\n   127→        // still, we 138→/// Persisted event stream; provides idempotent append + read access.\n   139→/// Uses Postgres JSONB for flexible schema evolution.\n   140→///\n   141→/// Table DDL (run via migrations):\n   142→/// ```sql\n   143→/// CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_store (\n   144→///     id           UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n   145→///     occurred_at  TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,\n   146→/// EventStore {\n   155→    pub async fn new(db_url: &str, max_connections: u32) -> Result<Self, sqlx::Error> {\n   156→        let pool = PgPoolOptions::new()\n   157→            .max_connections(max_connections)\n   158→            .connect(db_url)\n   159→            .await?;\n   160→        Ok(Self { pool })\n   161→    }\n   162→\n   163→    /// Append envelope to store.\n occurred_at, event_name, payload)\n   168→            VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)\n   169→            \"#,\n   170→            env.id,\n   171→            env.occurred_at,\n   172→            env.event.name(),\n   173→            serde_json::to_value(&env.event)?\n   174→        )\n   175→        .execute(&self.pool)\n   176→        .await?;\n   177→        Ok(())\n   178→    }\n   179→\n   180→    /// Read events by name—useful for rebuilding read models.\n   181→    pub async fn load_by_event(\n   182→        &self,\n   183→        event_name: &str,\n   184→    ) -> event,\n   212→                })\n   213→            })\n   214→            .collect()\n   215→    }\n   216→}\n   217→\n   218→/// Error type covering all repository/service error cases.\n   219→#[derive(Error, Debug)]\n   220→pub enum ServiceError {\n   221→    #[error(\"Database error: {0}\")]\n   222→    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n   223→\n   224→    #[error(\"Event serialisation   261→}\n   262→\n   263→impl PgBadgeRepository {\n   264→    pub fn new(pool: PgPool) -> Self {\n   265→        Self { pool }\n   266→    }\n   267→}\n   268→\n   269→#[async_trait]\n   270→impl BadgeRepository for PgBadgeRepository {\n   271→    async fn learner_already_has_badge(\n   272→        &self,\n   273→        learner_id: Uuid,\n   274→        badge_id: Uuid,\n   275→    ) -> badge_id\n   286→        )\n   287→        .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n   288→        .await?;\n   289→\n   290→        Ok(res.unwrap_or(false))\n   291→    }\n   292→\n   293→    async fn assign_badge(\n   294→        &self,\n   295→        learner_id: Uuid,\n   296→        badge: &BadgeAwarded,\n   297→    ) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n   298→        sqlx::query!(\n   299→            r#\"\n   300→   }\n   312→}\n   313→\n   314→/// Business logic deciding whether a quiz submission qualifies\n   315→/// for a badge. “Quiz Master” badge (UUID hard-coded)  \n   320→/// – Score exactly 100 ⇒ special “Perfect Score” badge\n   321→pub struct BadgeAwardService<R: BadgeRepository + 'static> {\n   322→    repo: Arc<R>,\n   323→    dispatcher: Dispatcher,\n   324→    event_store: Arc<EventStore>,\n   325→    subscribed_badges: HashSet<Uuid>,\n   326→}\n   327→\n   328→impl<R: BadgeRepository + 'static>   // Hard-coded UUIDs of known badges.\n   335→        let subscribed_badges = vec![\n   336→            // “Quiz Master”\n   337→            Uuid::parse_str(\"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\").unwrap(),\n   338→            // “Perfect Score”\n   339→            Uuid::parse_str(\"22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222\").unwrap(),\n   340→        ]\n   341→        .into_iter()\n   342→        .collect();\n   343→\n   344→        Self {\n   345→            repo,\n   346→            dispatcher,\n   347→            event_store,\n   348→            subscribed_badges,\n   349→        }\n   350→    }\n   351→\n   352→    /// Spawn the service on the Tokio runtime; returns its `JoinHandle`\n   353→    /// so callers can `.await` graceful shutdown.\n   354→    pub fn spawn(self: Arc<Self>) -> JoinHandle<()> {\n   355→        let mut rx = self.dispatcher.subscribe();\n   356→\n   357→        tokio::spawn(async move {\n   358→            loop {\n   359→                select! {\n   360→                    biased;\n   361→\n   362→                    // React to new events\n   363→                    msg = rx.recv() => match msg {\n   364→                        Ok(env) => {\n   365→                            if let Err(e) = self.process_event(env).await {\n   366→                                tracing::error!(\"BadgeAwardService failed: {e}\");\n   367→                            }\n   368→                        },\n   369→                        Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => {\n   370→                            tracing::warn!(\"Dispatcher closed, BadgeAwardService exiting.\");\n   371→                            break;\n   372→                        },\n   373→                        Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(skipped)) => {\n   374→                            tracing::warn!(\"BadgeAwardService lagged, skipped {skipped} messages\");\n   375→                        },\n   376→                    },\n   377→\n   378→                    // Allow cooperative cancellation.\n   379→                    _ = tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(60)) => {},\n   380→                }\n   381→            }\n   382→        })\n   383→    }\n   384→\n   385→    async fn process_event(&self, env: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n   386→        match env.event {\n   387→            DomainEvent::QuizSubmitted(sub) => self.maybe_award_badge(sub).await?,\n   388→            _ => { /* ignore */ }\n   389→        }\n   390→        Ok(())\n   391→    }\n   392→\n   393→    async fn maybe_award_badge(&self, sub: QuizSubmitted) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n   394→        // Determine qualifying badge.\n   395→        let (badge_id, reason) = if (sub.score - 100.0).abs() < f32::EPSILON {\n   396→            (\n   397→                Uuid::parse_str(\"22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222\").unwrap(),\n   398→                \"Perfect quiz score!\".to_owned(),\n   399→            )\n   400→        } else if sub.score >= 90.0 {\n   401→            (\n   402→                Uuid::parse_str(\"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\").unwrap(),\n   403→                \"Scored 90% or above on a quiz.\".to_owned(),\n   404→            )\n   405→        } else {\n   406→            return Ok(()); // Not eligible\n   407→        };\n   408→\n   409→        // Filter unknown badge IDs (forward-compat).\n   410→        if !self.subscribed_badges.contains(&badge_id) {\n   411→            return Ok(());\n   412→        }\n   413→\n   414→        // Idempotency check: learner may resubmit quiz multiple times.\n   415→        if self\n   416→            .repo\n   417→            .learner_already_has_badge(sub.learner_id, badge_id)\n   418→            .await?\n   419→        {\n   420→            return Ok(());\n   421→        }\n   422→\n   423→        // Compose and persist badge award.\n   424→        let badge = BadgeAwarded {\n   425→            badge_id,\n   426→            learner_id: sub.learner_id,\n   427→            awarded_at: Utc::now(),\n   428→            reason,\n   429→        };\n   430→\n   431→        self.repo.assign_badge(sub.learner_id, &badge).await?;\n   432→\n   433→        let envelope = EventEnvelope::new(DomainEvent::BadgeAwarded(badge.clone()));\n   434→        self.event_store.append(&envelope).await?;\n   435→        self.dispatcher.publish(envelope).await;\n   436→\n   437→        tracing::info!(\n   438→            learner=%sub.learner_id,\n   439→            badge=%badge.badge_id,\n   440→            \"Badge awarded!\"\n   441→        );\n   442→\n   443→        Ok(())\n   444→    }\n   445→}\n   446→\n   447→// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   448→//  Bootstrap / Example\n   449→// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   450→\n   451→/// Start an in-process example—useful when the module is compiled\n   452→/// as `cargo run --example dotenvy::dotenv().ok();\n   458→    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();\n   459→\n   460→    let db_url = std::env::var(\"DATABASE_URL\")\n   461→        .expect(\"DATABASE_URL must be set (e.g. 5).await?);\n   465→    let dispatcher = Dispatcher::new(1024);\n   466→\n   467→    let badge_repo = Arc::new(PgBadgeRepository::new(event_store.pool.clone()));\n   468→\n   469→    // ── Start demo_event = EventEnvelope::new(DomainEvent::QuizSubmitted(\n   479→        QuizSubmitted {\n   480→            quiz_id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n   481→            learner_id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n   482→ An event–driven badge–awarding micro-service for the\n//! “EduPulse Live – Event-Driven Social Learning Hub” platform.\n//!\n//! This module demonstrates how the core application publishes\n//! domain events while an out-of-process, autonomous component\n//! (here: `BadgeAwardService`) consumes them, performs business\n//! logic, persists the result and publishes its own follow-up\n//! events.  \n//!\n//! The service is completely self-contained: it hosts an in-memory\n//! dispatcher for local development, yet persists events and state\n//! in Postgres so it can be moved into a distributed setting\n//! later on (e.g. with NATS/Kafka‐backed `Dispatcher`).\n//!\n//! Architectural patterns shown here:\n//!   • Repository Pattern      – decoupling persistence\n//!   • Service Layer           – `BadgeAwardService`\n//!   • Event Sourcing          – immutable `EventStore`\n//!   • Async Rust w/ Tokio     – non-blocking I/O\n//!   • Error Handling w/ thiserror + anyhow\n//!\n//!   QuizSubmitted(QuizSubmitted),\n    BadgeAwarded(BadgeAwarded),\n    // … more variants omitted for brevity\n}\n\nimpl DomainEvent {\n    /// Helper returning event name for logging / metrics.\n   }\n}\n\n/// Metadata common to all envelopes—separates immutable event data\n/// from peripheral transport concerns such as IDs and timestamps.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct EventEnvelope {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub occurred_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub event: DomainEvent,\n}\n\nimpl EventEnvelope {\n    pub fn new(event: DomainEvent) -> Self {\n        Self {\n might replace the implementation with NATS,\n/// Kafka, RabbitMQ… etc., leaving the public API intact.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Dispatcher {\n    sender: broadcast::Sender<EventEnvelope>,\n}\n\nimpl Dispatcher {\n    /// Create a new dispatcher with a configurable ring buffer size.\n    pub fn new(channel_size: usize) -> Self {\n        let (sender, _rx) = broadcast::channel(channel_size);\n        Self { sender }\n    }\n\n    /// Publish an envelope to all subscribers.\n    pub async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) {\n        // broadcast::Sender::send is synchronous but inexpensive.\n        // Failure only occurs when there are no active receivers;\n        // still, we ignore that because the event is persisted in\n        // the store before reaching this point.\n        let _ = self.sender.send(envelope);\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to a continuous stream of events.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<EventEnvelope> {\n        self.sender.subscribe()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Persisted event stream; provides idempotent append + read access.\n/// Uses Postgres JSONB for flexible schema evolution.\n///\n/// Table DDL (run via migrations):\n/// ```sql\n/// CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS event_store (\n///     id           UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n///     occurred_at  TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE        Ok(Self { pool })\n    }\n\n    /// Append envelope to store.\n    pub async fn append(&self, env: &EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), sqlx::Error> {\n        sqlx::query!(\n            r#\"\n read models.\n    pub async fn load_by_event(\n        &self,\n        event_name: &str,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<EventEnvelope>, sqlx::Error> {\n        let recs = sqlx::query!(\n            r#\"\n {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"Internal channel closed\")]\n    ChannelClosed,\n}\n\n/// Repository abstraction for badge persistence.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait            FROM learner_badges\n                WHERE learner_id = $1 AND badge_id = $2\n            ) AS \"exists!\"\n            \"#,\n            learner_id,\n            badge_id\n        )\n }\n}\n\n/// Business logic deciding whether a quiz submission qualifies\n/// for a badge.  event_store: Arc<EventStore>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        // Hard-coded UUIDs of known badges.\n        let subscribed_badges = vec![\n            // “Quiz Master”\n .into_iter()\n        .collect();\n\n        Self {\n            repo,\n            dispatcher,\n            event_store,\n            subscribed_badges,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Spawn the service self.dispatcher.subscribe();\n\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            loop {\n                select! {\n                    biased;\n\n                    // React to new events\n                    msg = rx.recv() => match msg {\n                        Ok(env) => {\n                            if let Err(e) = self.process_event(env).await {\n           tracing::warn!(\"Dispatcher closed, BadgeAwardService exiting.\");\n                            break;\n                        },\n                        Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(skipped)) => {\n                            tracing::warn!(\"BadgeAwardService lagged, skipped {skipped} messages\");\n                        },\n                    },\n\n                    // Allow cooperative cancellation.\n                    _ = tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(60)) => {},\n                }\n            }\n        })\n    }\n\n    async fn process_event(&self, env: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n        match env.event {\n            DomainEvent::QuizSubmitted(sub) => self.maybe_award_badge(sub).await?,\n            _ => { /* ignore */ }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn maybe_award_badge(&self, sub: QuizSubmitted) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n        // Determine qualifying badge.\n        let (badge_id, reason) = if (sub.score - 100.0).abs() < f32::EPSILON {\n            (\n                Uuid::parse_str(\"22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222\").unwrap(),\n                \"Perfect quiz score!\".to_owned(),\n            )\n        } else if sub.score >= 90.0 {\n            (\n                Uuid::parse_str(\"11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111\").unwrap(),\n                \"Scored 90% or above on a quiz.\".to_owned(),\n            )\n        } else {\n            return Ok(()); // Not eligible\n        };\n\n        // Filter unknown badge IDs (forward-compat).\n        if !self.subscribed_badges.contains(&badge_id) {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n\n        // Idempotency check: learner may resubmit quiz multiple times.\n        if self\n            .repo\n            .learner_already_has_badge(sub.learner_id, badge_id)\n            .await?\n        {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n\n        // Compose and persist badge award.\n self.repo.assign_badge(sub.learner_id, &badge).await?;\n\n        let envelope = EventEnvelope::new(DomainEvent::BadgeAwarded(badge.clone()));\n        self.event_store.append(&envelope).await?;\n        self.dispatcher.publish(envelope).await;\n\n        tracing::info!(\n            learner=%sub.learner_id,\n            badge=%badge.badge_id,\n            \"Badge awarded!\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n//  Bootstrap / Example\n// 363→}\n   364→\n   365→/// Repeatedly polls the outbox and hands events to the broker.\n   366→///\n   367→/// Spawn via [`tokio::spawn`]; it terminates when the passed `shutdown`\n   368→/// [`tokio::sync::broadcast::Receiver`] is signalled.\n   369→pub struct EventDispatcher<R: EventOutboxRepository, P: EventPublisher> {\n   370→    repo: Arc<R>,\n   371→    publisher: Arc<P>,\n   372→ }\n   394→\n   395→    #[instrument(skip(self, shutdown_rx), name = \"event_dispatch_loop\")]\n   396→    pub fn start(\n   397→        self,\n   398→        mut shutdown_rx:   _ = shutdown_rx.recv() => {\n   404→                        info!(\"EventDispatcher received shutdown signal\");\n   405→                        break;\n   406→                    }\n   407→                    _ = self.tick() => {}\n   408→                }\n   409→            }\n   410→        })\n   411→    }\n   412→\n   413→    /// Single poll → publish → ack iteration.\n   414→    async fn tick(&self) {\n   415→        match self\n   416→            .repo\n   417→            .fetch_pending(self.batch_size)\n   418→            .await\n   419→        {\n   420→            Ok(events) if events.iter().map(|e| e.id).collect();\n   425→                debug!(count = events.len(), \"Publishing events\");\n   426→\n   427→                // Publish concurrently but limit concurrency to avoid backpressure.\n   428→                let results = stream::iter(events)\n   429→                    .map(|e| async {\n   430→                        if let Err(err) = self.publisher.publish(&e).await {\n   431→                            error!(error = %err, \"Failed to publish event\");\n   432→                            Err(err)\n   433→                        } else {\n   434→                            Ok(())\n   435→                        }\n   436→                    })\n   437→                    .buffer_unordered(8)\n   438→                    .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n   439→                    .await;\n   440→\n   441→                if results.iter().all(Result::is_ok) {\n   442→                    if let Err(err) = self.repo.mark_dispatched(&ids).await {\n   443→                        error!(%err, \"Failed to mark events as dispatched\");\n   444→                    }\n   445→                } else {\n   446→                    warn!(\"Some events failed to publish; will retry later\");\n   447→                }\n   448→            }\n   449→            Err(err) => {\n   450→                error!(%err, \"Failed to fetch pending events\");\n   451→                sleep(self.poll_interval).await;\n   452→            }\n   453→        }\n   454→    }\n   455→}\n   456→\n   457→/* let repo = Arc::new(PgOutboxRepository::new(pool));\n   493→\n   494→    let publisher = Arc::new(\n   495→        NatsPublisher::connect(nats_url, nats_jwt, \"edupulse\")\n   496→            .await\n   497→            .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(e))?,\n   498→    );\n   499→\n   500→    let dispatcher = EventDispatcher::new(\n   501→        repo,\n   502→        publisher,\n   503→        100,\n Each bounded context persists `DomainEvent`s to its local\n//! PostgreSQL **outbox** table as part of the same transaction that mutates\n//! aggregates.  A dedicated background task (see [`EventDispatcher`]) then\n//! reliably publishes those events to the message broker (NATS) and marks them\n//! as dispatched, guaranteeing at-least-once delivery while preserving\n//! transactional integrity.\n//!\n//! let Err(e) = res {\n                        error!(error = ?e, \"Worker crashed\");\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Fetches pending events and sends them to the worker channel.\n    #[instrument(skip_all, fields(batch_size = BATCH_SIZE))]\n    async fn fetch_pending(&self, tx_chan: &Sender<EventEnvelope>) -> Result<usize, OutboxError> {\n outbox\n            SET locked_until = NOW() + INTERVAL '1 minute'\n            WHERE id IN (\n                SELECT id\n                FROM outbox\n                WHERE delivered_at IS NULL\n };\n\n            tx_chan.send(envelope).await.map_err(|_| OutboxError::ChannelClosed)?;\n        }\n\n        Ok(count)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Background worker that receives envelopes and publishes them       error!(worker = idx, event_id = %envelope.id, error = ?e, \"Failed to publish event\");\n                if let Err(db_err) = increment_retries(envelope.id).await {\n                    error!(error = ?db_err, \"Failed to increment retry counter\");\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Updates `delivered_at` timestamp.\nasync fn mark_delivered(event_id: Uuid) Modules\n    10→//! -------\n    11→//! • `id`          – monotonic, roughly-sortable identifiers (Snowflake-ish)\n    12→//! • `time`        – thin wrappers around `chrono` for consistent timekeeping\n    13→//! • `crypto`      – secure random token generation + constant-time equality\n    14→//! • `retry`       – async, cancel-safe retry / back-off combinator\n    15→//! • `event`       – light-weight domain-event abstraction + envelope\n    16→//! • `serde_ext`   – (de)serialization helpers\n    17→//! • `telemetry`   – opinionated tracing / log initialisation\n    18→//!\n    19→//! Monotonically increasing 64-bit identifiers inspired by Twitter’s Snowflake\n    42→    //! algorithm.  IDs are roughly sortable by creation time while remaining unique\n    43→    //! across workers.  \n    44→    //!\n    45→    //! Layout (big-endian):\n    46→    //! ┌────────41b────────┬────10b────┬───12b─┐\n    47→    //! │ milliseconds ts   │ worker_id │ seq  │\n    48→    //! └────────────────────┴───────────┴──────┘\n    49→\n    50→    use parking_lot::Mutex;\n    51→    use std::{sync::Arc, time::SystemTime};\n    52→\n    53→    // Jan 1st 2020 00:00:00.000 UTC (chosen arbitrarily, but in the past)\n    54→    const CUSTOM_EPOCH_MILLIS: u64 = 1_577_836_800_000;\n    55→\n    56→    /// Returned whenever the clock goes *backwards* (NTP sync, leap second,\n    57→    /// manual change, etc.) and we cannot guarantee monotonicity.\n    58→    #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]\n    59→    #[error(\"system clock drifted backwards\")]\n    60→    pub struct ClockDriftError;\n    61→\n    62→    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    63→    pub struct SnowflakeIdGenerator {\n    64→        inner: Arc<Mutex<State>>,\n    65→        worker_id: u16,\n    66→    }\n    67→\n    68→    #[derive(Debug)]\n    69→    struct State {\n    70→        last_ts: u64,\n    71→        sequence: u16,\n    72→    }\n    73→\n    74→    impl SnowflakeIdGenerator {\n    75→        /// Max value that can fit into the *worker* field (10 bits)\n    76→        pub const MAX_WORKER_ID: u16 = (1 << 10) - 1;\n    77→\n    78→        /// Create a new generator for the specified `worker_id`\n    79→        pub fn new(worker_id: u16) -> Self {\n    80→            assert!(\n    81→                worker_id <= Self::MAX_WORKER_ID,\n    82→                \"worker_id {} exceeds max {}\",\n    83→                worker_id,\n    84→                Self::MAX_WORKER_ID\n    85→            );\n    86→\n    87→            Self {\n    88→                inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(State {\n    89→                    last_ts: 0,\n    90→                    sequence: 0,\n    91→                })),\n    92→                worker_id,\n    93→            }\n    94→        }\n    95→\n    96→        /// Generate the next 64-bit identifier.\n    97→        ///\n    98→        /// NOTE: This operation grabs a MLS; however contention should be\n    99→        /// minimal because the critical section is extremely small.\n   100→        pub fn next_id(&self) -> Result<u64, ClockDriftError> {\n   101→            const SEQ_MASK: u16 = (1 << 12) - 1; // 12-bit mask\n   102→\n   103→            let mut state = self.inner.lock();\n   104→            let mut ts = current_time_millis();\n   105→\n   106→            if ts < state.last_ts {\n   107→                // Time went backwards\n   108→                return Err(ClockDriftError);\n   109→            }\n   110→\n   111→            if ts == state.last_ts {\n   112→                // Same millisecond ➜ bump sequence\n   113→                state.sequence = (state.sequence + 1) & SEQ_MASK;\n   114→                if state.sequence == 0 {\n   115→                    // Sequence overflow ➜ wait for next millisecond\n   116→                    ts = spin_next_millis(state.last_ts);\n   117→                }\n   118→            } else {\n   119→                state.sequence = 0;\n   120→            }\n   121→\n   122→            state.last_ts = ts;\n   123→\n   124→            let id = ((ts - Collection of low-level helper utilities used across the EduPulse Live\n//! backend.  These helpers stay intentionally *domain-agnostic* so that they\n//! can be re-used by service‐layer, repository, or background-worker crates\n//! without incurring cyclic-dependency hell.\n//!\n//! Modules\n//! -------\n//! • `id`          – monotonic, roughly-sortable identifiers (Snowflake-ish)\n//! • `time`        – thin wrappers around `chrono` for consistent timekeeping\n//! • `crypto`      – secure random token generation + constant-time equality\n//! • `retry`       – async, cancel-safe retry / back-off combinator\n//! • `event`       – light-weight domain-event abstraction + envelope\n//! • `serde_ext`   – (de)serialization helpers\n//! • `telemetry`   – opinionated tracing / log initialisation\n//!\n//! The code is `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` by default when compiled with\n//! `--cfg edu_pulse_deny_unsafe`, giving defence-in-depth for our audit\n//! requirements.\n\n#![cfg_attr(edu_pulse_deny_unsafe, forbid(unsafe_code))]\n\npub mod id;\npub mod time;\npub mod crypto;\npub mod retry;\npub mod event;\npub mod serde_ext;\npub mod telemetry;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: id\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\npub mod id {\n    //! Monotonically increasing 64-bit identifiers inspired by Twitter’s Snowflake\n    //! algorithm.  IDs are roughly sortable by creation time while remaining unique\n    //! across workers.  \n    //!\n    //! Layout (big-endian):\n    //! ┌────────41b────────┬────10b────┬───12b─┐\n    //! │ milliseconds ts   │ worker_id │ seq  │\n    //! └────────────────────┴───────────┴──────┘\n\n    use parking_lot::Mutex;\n    use std::{sync::Arc, time::SystemTime};\n\n    // Jan 1st 2020 00:00:00.000 UTC (chosen arbitrarily, but in the past)\n    const CUSTOM_EPOCH_MILLIS: u64 = 1_577_836_800_000;\n\n    /// Returned whenever the clock goes *backwards* (NTP sync, leap second,\n    /// manual change, etc.) and we cannot guarantee monotonicity.\n    #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]\n    #[error(\"system clock drifted backwards\")]\n    pub struct ClockDriftError;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    pub struct SnowflakeIdGenerator {\n        inner: Arc<Mutex<State>>,\n        worker_id: u16,\n    }\n\n    #[derive(Debug)]\n    struct State {\n        last_ts: u64,\n        sequence: u16,\n    }\n\n    impl SnowflakeIdGenerator {\n        /// Max value that can fit into the *worker* field (10 bits)\n        pub const MAX_WORKER_ID: u16 = (1 << 10) - 1;\n\n        /// Create a new generator for the specified `worker_id`\n        pub fn new(worker_id: u16) -> Self {\n            assert!(\n                worker_id <= Self::MAX_WORKER_ID,\n                \"worker_id {} exceeds max {}\",\n                worker_id,\n                Self::MAX_WORKER_ID\n            );\n\n            Self {\n                inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(State {\n                    last_ts: 0,\n                    sequence: 0,\n                })),\n                worker_id,\n            }\n        }\n\n        /// Generate the next 64-bit identifier.\n        ///\n        /// NOTE: This operation grabs a MLS; however contention should be\n        /// minimal because the critical section is extremely small.\n        pub fn next_id(&self) -> Result<u64, ClockDriftError> {\n            const SEQ_MASK: u16 = (1 << 12) - 1; // 12-bit mask\n\n            let mut state = self.inner.lock();\n            let mut ts = current_time_millis();\n\n            if ts < state.last_ts {\n                // Time went backwards\n                return Err(ClockDriftError);\n            }\n\n            if ts == state.last_ts {\n                // Same millisecond ➜ bump sequence\n                state.sequence = (state.sequence + 1) & SEQ_MASK;\n                if state.sequence == 0 {\n                    // Sequence overflow ➜ wait for next millisecond\n                    ts = spin_next_millis(state.last_ts);\n                }\n            } else {\n                state.sequence = 0;\n            }\n\n            state.last_ts = ts;\n\n            let id = ((ts - CUSTOM_EPOCH_MILLIS) << 22)  // 41 bits << (10+12)\n                | ((self.worker_id as u64) << 12)        // 10 bits << 12\n                | (state.sequence as u64);               // 12 bits\n\n            Ok(id)\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[inline(always)]\n    fn current_time_millis() -> u64 {\n        SystemTime::now()\n            .duration_since(SystemTime::UNIX_EPOCH)\n            .expect(\"time before unix epoch\")\n            .as_millis() as u64\n    }\n\n    /// Busy-wait until we reach a new millisecond\n    fn spin_next_millis(last_ts: u64) -> u64 {\n        let mut ts = current_time_millis();\n        while ts <= last_ts {\n            std::hint::spin_loop();\n            ts = current_time_millis();\n        }\n        ts\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: time\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\npub mod time {\n    //! ```\n\n    use rand::Rng;\n    use std::time::Duration;\n    use tokio::time::sleep;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone)]\n    pub struct RetryPolicy {\n        pub max_retries: usize,\n        pub initial_delay: Duration,\n        pub max_delay: Duration,\n    }\n\n    impl RetryPolicy {\n        pub fn bounded(max_retries: usize) -> Self {\n            Self {\n                max_retries,\n                initial_delay: Duration::from_millis(100),\n                max_delay: Duration::from_secs(5),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Retry the async `operation` according to `policy`.\n    ///\n    /// The `operation` closure is re-invoked if it returns `Err`.\n    pub async fn retry_async<O, Op, E, T>(\n        policy: RetryPolicy,\n        mut operation: O,\n    ) -> Result<T, E>\n    where\n        O: FnMut() -> Op,\n        Op: std::future::Future<Output = Result<T, E>>,\n    {\n        let mut attempt: usize = 0;\n        let mut delay = policy.initial_delay;\n\n        loop {\n            match operation().await {\n                ok @ Ok(_) => return ok,\n                Err(err) if attempt >= policy.max_retries => return Err(err),\n                Err(_) => {\n                    // full-jitter back-off\n                    let sleep_ms = rand::thread_rng().gen_range(0..delay.as_millis() as u64 + 1);\n                    sleep(Duration::from_millis(sleep_ms)).await;\n                    delay = std::cmp::min(delay * 2, policy.max_delay);\n                    attempt += 1;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: event\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\npub mod event {\n    //! ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n    66→\n    67→/// Very small, in-process, fan-out event bus used exclusively for testing.\n    68→///\n    69→/// Every call to `subscribe()` registers a new channel (Sender) that will\n    70→/// receive a **copy** of every published DomainEvent.  Publication errors are\n    71→/// silently swallowed by individual subscribers so that one slow consumer\n    72→/// never blocks the rest.\n    73→#[derive(Clone, Default)]\n    74→struct EventBus {\n    75→    subscribers: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Sender<DomainEvent>>>>,\n    76→}\n    77→\n    78→impl EventBus {\n    79→    /// Create a fresh, empty bus.\n    80→    fn new() -> Self {\n    81→        Self::default()\n    82→    }\n    83→\n    84→    /// Publish an event to all currently active subscribers.\n    85→    fn publish(&self, event: DomainEvent) -> Result<(), BusError> {\n    86→  92→            // Ignore per-subscriber errors; fire-and-forget semantics.\n    93→            let _ = tx.send(event.clone());\n    94→        }\n    95→        Ok(())\n    96→    }\n    97→\n    98→ // URL safe\n\n        // Extend until we reach the requested length\n        while token.len() < target_len {\n            token.push_str(\n                &base64::encode(Uuid::new_v4().as_bytes())\n                    .replace(['+', '/', '='], \"\"),\n            );\n        }\n        token.truncate(target_len);\n        Ok(token)\n    }\n\n    // Centralized Event Publishing Service for EduPulse Live\n//! =======================================================\n//!\n//! This module provides a unified, generic event publishing service that\n//! consolidates the previously scattered event publishing logic from various\n//! modules (module_15, module_48, module_77). It eliminates code duplication\n//! related to event serialization, connection handling, and error management.\n//!\n//! # Design\n//!\n//! The `EventPublisher` struct manages the connection configuration to the\n//! event bus and provides a generic `publish` method that can serialize and\n//! send any payload type that implements `serde::Serialize + Sync`.\n//!\n//! # Error Handling\n//!\n//! A custom `EventPublisherError` enum provides standardized error reporting\n//! for all event publishing operations, covering serialization failures,\n//! connection issues, and send failures.\n//!\n//! # Usage\n//!\n//! ```rust,no_run\n//! use crate::event_publisher::{EventPublisher, EventPublisherError};\n//! use serde::Serialize;\n//!\n//! #[derive(Serialize)]\n//! struct MyEvent {\n//!     user_id: u64,\n//!     action: String,\n//! }\n//!\n//! async fn example() -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n//!     let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n//!     let event = MyEvent {\n//!         user_id: 42,\n//!         action: \"login\".to_string(),\n//!     };\n//!     publisher.publish(&event).await\n//! }\n//! ```\n\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\nuse crate::utils::event_bus::{get_event_bus_config, send_event_to_stream, EventBusConfig, EventStreamError};\n\n/// Errors that can occur during event publishing.\n///\n/// This enum consolidates all error cases from the previously scattered\n/// event publishing implementations across different modules.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EventPublisherError {\n    /// Failed to serialize the event payload to JSON.\n    #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Failed to connect to the event bus.\n    #[error(\"Connection error: {0}\")]\n    Connection(String),\n\n    /// Failed to send the event to the stream.\n    #[error(\"Send error: {0}\")]\n    Send(String),\n\n    /// Operation timed out.\n    #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n    Timeout(u64),\n}\n\nimpl From<EventStreamError> for EventPublisherError {\n    fn from(err: EventStreamError) -> Self {\n        match err {\n            EventStreamError::ConnectionFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Connection(msg),\n            EventStreamError::SendFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Send(msg),\n            EventStreamError::Timeout(ms) => EventPublisherError::Timeout(ms),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Centralized event publisher for the EduPulse Live platform.\n///\n/// This struct manages the event bus configuration and provides a single,\n/// generic method for publishing events of any serializable type.\n///\n/// # Thread Safety\n///\n/// `EventPublisher` is `Send + Sync` and can be safely shared across threads\n/// using `Arc<EventPublisher>`.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EventPublisher {\n    config: EventBusConfig,\n}\n\nimpl EventPublisher {\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` instance.\n    ///\n    /// Retrieves configuration from the `get_event_bus_config()` function\n    /// in `crate::utils`, which reads from environment variables or uses\n    /// sensible defaults.\n    ///\n    /// # Example\n    ///\n    /// ```rust,no_run\n    /// let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n    /// ```\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let config = get_event_bus_config();\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` with a custom configuration.\n    ///\n    /// This is useful for testing or when you need to override the default\n    /// configuration.\n    pub fn with_config(config: EventBusConfig) -> Self {\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This method handles:\n    /// - Serialization of the payload to JSON\n    /// - Sending the serialized event to the event stream\n    /// - Error handling and propagation\n    ///\n    /// # Type Parameters\n    ///\n    /// * `T` - The event payload type. Must implement `Serialize` and `Sync`.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    ///\n    /// * `payload` - A reference to the event payload to publish.\n    ///\n    /// # Returns\n    ///\n    /// Returns `Ok(())` if the event was successfully published, or an\n    /// `EventPublisherError` if serialization or sending failed.\n    ///\n    /// # Example\n    ///\n    /// ```rust,no_run\n    /// use serde::Serialize;\n    ///\n    /// #[derive(Serialize)]\n    /// struct UserLoggedIn {\n    ///     user_id: u64,\n    ///     timestamp: i64,\n    /// }\n    ///\n    /// async fn publish_login(publisher: &EventPublisher, user_id: u64) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n    ///     let event = UserLoggedIn {\n    ///         user_id,\n    ///         timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),\n    ///     };\n    ///     publisher.publish(&event).await\n    /// }\n    /// ```\n    pub async fn publish<T: Serialize + Sync>(&self, payload: &T) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        // Serialize the payload to JSON\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n\n        // Determine the topic based on the config\n        let topic = format!(\"{}.events\", self.config.topic_prefix);\n\n        // Send the event to the stream\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to a specific topic on the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This variant allows specifying a custom topic name, which is useful\n    /// when different event types should be routed to different topics.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    ///\n    /// * `topic` - The topic name to publish to.\n    /// * `payload` - A reference to the event payload to publish.\n    ///\n    /// # Returns\n    ///\n    /// Returns `Ok(())` if the event was successfully published, or an\n    /// `EventPublisherError` if serialization or sending failed.\n    pub async fn publish_to_topic<T: Serialize + Sync>(\n        &self,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &T,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        // Serialize the payload to JSON\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n\n        // Build the full topic path\n        let full_topic = format!(\"{}.{}\", self.config.topic_prefix, topic);\n\n        // Send the event to the stream\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &full_topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %full_topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a reference to the current configuration.\n    pub fn config(&self) -> &EventBusConfig {\n        &self.config\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for EventPublisher {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::Serialize;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\n    struct TestEvent {\n        id: u64,\n        message: String,\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_event() {\n        let publisher = Current Event Publishing Logic\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_48.txt\n// Lines: 304-351\n\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher: Send + Sync {\n    async fn publish(&self, event: &EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), PublishError>;\n}\n\npub struct NatsPublisher {\n    conn: Connection,\n    subject_prefix: String,\n}\n\nimpl NatsPublisher {\n    pub async fn connect(\n        nats_url: &str,\n        jwt: Option<String>,\n        prefix: impl Into<String>,\n    ) -> Result<Self, PublishError> {\n        // ... connection logic\n    }\n\n    fn subject_for(&self, event_type: &str) -> String {\n        format!(\"{}.{}\", self.subject_prefix, event_type)\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EventPublisher for NatsPublisher {\n    #[instrument(skip(self, event))]\n    async fn publish(&self, event: &EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), PublishError> {\n        let subject = self.subject_for(&event.event_type);\n        let data = serde_json::to_vec(event)?;  // Serialization here\n        self.conn.publish(subject, data).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Module 77 - Current Event Publishing Logic\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_77.txt\n// Lines: 191-208\n\n/// Contract for anything that can publish events from the outbox to an external\n/// broker (NATS, RabbitMQ, Kafka, …).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher {\n    async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), anyhow::Error>;\n}\n\n/// Mock publisher for unit-testing – prints events to the console.\npub struct StdoutPublisher;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EventPublisher for StdoutPublisher {\n    #[instrument(level = \"info\", skip(self))]\n    async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), anyhow::Error> {\n        println!(\"🔔 Publishing event: {envelope:#?}\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Duplicated Code Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Event Serialization Logic**: Each module independently serializes events using `serde_json`:\n   - module_48 uses `serde_json::to_vec(event)?`\n   - module_77's `OutboxRow::from_envelope` uses `serde_json::to_value(&envelope.event)?`\n   - module_15 serializes via `serde_json::to_value(&env.event)?` in EventStore\n\n2. **Error Handling**: Each module defines its own error types:\n   - module_15: `ServiceError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_48: `PublishError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_77: `OutboxError` enum with `Serialization` variant\n\n3. **Connection/Configuration Management**: Each module manages its own broker connection:\n   - module_48: `NatsPublisher::connect()` with URL, JWT, prefix\n   - module_77: Uses environment variables via lazy static\n   - module_15: In-memory broadcast channel\n\n4. **Publisher Trait Definition**: Both module_48 and module_77 define their own `EventPublisher` trait with similar signatures but incompatible types.\n\n### Architectural Issues\n\n- **Code Duplication**: Serialization, error handling, and publishing logic repeated across modules\n- **Inconsistent Interfaces**: Different modules use different trait signatures and error types\n- **Tight Coupling**: Each module handles its own connection management\n- **Maintenance Burden**: Bug fixes or improvements need to be applied in multiple places\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. New File: /app/project/src/event_publisher.txt\n\n```rust\n//! src/event_publisher.rs\n//!\n//! Centralized Event Publishing Service for EduPulse Live\n//! =======================================================\n//!\n//! This module provides a unified, generic event publishing service that\n//! consolidates the previously scattered event publishing logic from various\n//! modules (module_15, module_48, module_77). It eliminates code duplication\n//! related to event serialization, connection handling, and error management.\n\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\nuse crate::utils::event_bus::{get_event_bus_config, send_event_to_stream, EventBusConfig, EventStreamError};\n\n/// Errors that can occur during event publishing.\n///\n/// This enum consolidates all error cases from the previously scattered\n/// event publishing implementations across different modules.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EventPublisherError {\n    /// Failed to serialize the event payload to JSON.\n    #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Failed to connect to the event bus.\n    #[error(\"Connection error: {0}\")]\n    Connection(String),\n\n    /// Failed to send the event to the stream.\n    #[error(\"Send error: {0}\")]\n    Send(String),\n\n    /// Operation timed out.\n    #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n    Timeout(u64),\n}\n\nimpl From<EventStreamError> for EventPublisherError {\n    fn from(err: EventStreamError) -> Self {\n        match err {\n            EventStreamError::ConnectionFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Connection(msg),\n            EventStreamError::SendFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Send(msg),\n            EventStreamError::Timeout(ms) => EventPublisherError::Timeout(ms),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Centralized event publisher for the EduPulse Live platform.\n///\n/// This struct manages the event bus configuration and provides a single,\n/// generic method for publishing events of any serializable type.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EventPublisher {\n    config: EventBusConfig,\n}\n\nimpl EventPublisher {\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` instance.\n    ///\n    /// Retrieves configuration from the `get_event_bus_config()` function\n    /// in `crate::utils::event_bus`.\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let config = get_event_bus_config();\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` with a custom configuration.\n    pub fn with_config(config: EventBusConfig) -> Self {\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This method handles:\n    /// - Serialization of the payload to JSON\n    /// - Sending the serialized event to the event stream\n    /// - Error handling and propagation\n    ///\n    /// # Type Parameters\n    ///\n    /// * `T` - The event payload type. Must implement `Serialize` and `Sync`.\n    pub async fn publish<T: Serialize + Sync>(&self, payload: &T) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        // Serialize the payload to JSON\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n\n        // Determine the topic based on the config\n        let topic = format!(\"{}.events\", self.config.topic_prefix);\n\n        // Send the event to the stream\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to a specific topic on the event stream.\n    pub async fn publish_to_topic<T: Serialize + Sync>(\n        &self,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &T,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n        let full_topic = format!(\"{}.{}\", self.config.topic_prefix, topic);\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &full_topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %full_topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a reference to the current configuration.\n    pub fn config(&self) -> &EventBusConfig {\n        &self.config\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for EventPublisher {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::Serialize;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\n    struct TestEvent {\n        id: u64,\n        message: String,\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_event() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 1,\n            message: \"test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish(&event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_to_custom_topic() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 2,\n            message: \"custom topic test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish_to_topic(\"custom.topic\", &event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Modifications to /app/project/src/utils.txt\n\nAdd a new `event_bus` module before the `telemetry` module:\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/utils.txt\n// Add after line 335 (after the event module closing brace)\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: event_bus\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\npub mod event_bus {\n    //! Mock event bus infrastructure used by the centralized `EventPublisher` service.\n    //! In production these would connect to a real message broker like NATS or Kafka.\n\n    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n    /// Configuration for connecting to the event bus.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    pub struct EventBusConfig {\n        pub broker_url: String,\n        pub topic_prefix: String,\n        pub max_retries: u32,\n        pub timeout_ms: u64,\n    }\n\n    impl Default for EventBusConfig {\n        fn default() -> Self {\n            Self {\n                broker_url: \"nats://localhost:4222\".to_string(),\n                topic_prefix: \"edupulse\".to_string(),\n                max_retries: 3,\n                timeout_ms: 5000,\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieves the event bus configuration from environment or defaults.\n    pub fn get_event_bus_config() -> EventBusConfig {\n        EventBusConfig {\n            broker_url: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_URL\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"nats://localhost:4222\".to_string()),\n            topic_prefix: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_TOPIC_PREFIX\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"edupulse\".to_string()),\n            max_retries: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_MAX_RETRIES\")\n                .ok()\n                .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())\n                .unwrap_or(3),\n            timeout_ms: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_TIMEOUT_MS\")\n                .ok()\n                .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())\n                .unwrap_or(5000),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Error type for event stream operations.\n    #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]\n    pub enum EventStreamError {\n        #[error(\"Connection failed: {0}\")]\n        ConnectionFailed(String),\n        #[error(\"Send failed: {0}\")]\n        SendFailed(String),\n        #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n        Timeout(u64),\n    }\n\n    /// Sends an event payload (as a JSON string) to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This is a mock implementation that simulates sending events to a broker.\n    pub async fn send_event_to_stream(\n        config: &EventBusConfig,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &str,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventStreamError> {\n        tracing::debug!(\n            broker_url = %config.broker_url,\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_len = payload.len(),\n            \"Sending event to stream\"\n        );\n\n        // Simulate network latency\n        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1)).await;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. module_48 uses `serde_json::to_vec(event)?`\n   - module_77's `OutboxRow::from_envelope` uses `serde_json::to_value(&envelope.event)?`\n   - module_15 serializes via `serde_json::to_value(&env.event)?` in EventStore\n\n2. **Error Handling**: Each module defines its own error types:\n   - module_15: `ServiceError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_48: `PublishError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_77: `OutboxError` enum with `Serialization` variant\n\n3. **Connection/Configuration Management**: Each module manages its own broker connection:\n   - module_48: `NatsPublisher::connect()` with URL, JWT, prefix\n   - module_77: Uses environment variables via lazy static\n   - module_15: In-memory broadcast channel\n\n4. **Publisher Trait Definition**: Both module_48 and module_77 define their own `EventPublisher` trait with similar signatures but incompatible types.\n\n### Architectural Issues\n\n- **Code Duplication**: Serialization, error handling, and publishing logic repeated across modules\n- **Inconsistent Interfaces**: Different modules use different trait signatures and error types\n- **Tight Coupling**: Each module handles its own connection management\n- **Maintenance Burden**: Bug fixes or improvements need to be applied in multiple places\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. New File: /app/project/src/event_publisher.txt\n\n```rust\n//! src/event_publisher.rs\n//!\n//! Centralized Event Publishing Service for EduPulse Live\n//! =======================================================\n//!\n//! This module provides a unified, generic event publishing service that\n//! consolidates the previously scattered event publishing logic from various\n//! modules (module_15, module_48, module_77). It eliminates code duplication\n//! related to event serialization, connection handling, and error management.\n\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\nuse crate::utils::event_bus::{get_event_bus_config, send_event_to_stream, EventBusConfig, EventStreamError};\n\n/// Errors that can occur during event publishing.\n///\n/// This enum consolidates all error cases from the previously scattered\n/// event publishing implementations across different modules.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EventPublisherError {\n    /// Failed to serialize the event payload to JSON.\n    #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Failed to connect to the event bus.\n    #[error(\"Connection error: {0}\")]\n    Connection(String),\n\n    /// Failed to send the event to the stream.\n    #[error(\"Send error: {0}\")]\n    Send(String),\n\n    /// Operation timed out.\n    #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n    Timeout(u64),\n}\n\nimpl From<EventStreamError> for EventPublisherError {\n    fn from(err: EventStreamError) -> Self {\n        match err {\n            EventStreamError::ConnectionFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Connection(msg),\n            EventStreamError::SendFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Send(msg),\n            EventStreamError::Timeout(ms) => EventPublisherError::Timeout(ms),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Centralized event publisher for the EduPulse Live platform.\n///\n/// This struct manages the event bus configuration and provides a single,\n/// generic method for publishing events of any serializable type.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EventPublisher {\n    config: EventBusConfig,\n}\n\nimpl EventPublisher {\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` instance.\n    ///\n    /// Retrieves configuration from the `get_event_bus_config()` function\n    /// in `crate::utils::event_bus`.\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let config = get_event_bus_config();\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` with a custom configuration.\n    pub fn with_config(config: EventBusConfig) -> Self {\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This method handles:\n    /// - Serialization of the payload to JSON\n    /// - Sending the serialized event to the event stream\n    /// - Error handling and propagation\n    ///\n    /// # Type Parameters\n    ///\n    /// * `T` - The event payload type. Must implement `Serialize` and `Sync`.\n    pub async fn publish<T: Serialize + Sync>(&self, payload: &T) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        // Serialize the payload to JSON\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n\n        // Determine the topic based on the config\n        let topic = format!(\"{}.events\", self.config.topic_prefix);\n\n        // Send the event to the stream\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to a specific topic on the event stream.\n    pub async fn publish_to_topic<T: Serialize + Sync>(\n        &self,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &T,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n        let full_topic = format!(\"{}.{}\", self.config.topic_prefix, topic);\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &full_topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %full_topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a reference to the current configuration.\n    pub fn config(&self) -> &EventBusConfig {\n        &self.config\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for EventPublisher {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::Serialize;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\n    struct TestEvent {\n        id: u64,\n        message: String,\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_event() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 1,\n            message: \"test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish(&event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_to_custom_topic() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 2,\n            message: \"custom topic test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish_to_topic(\"custom.topic\", &event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. module_48 uses `serde_json::to_vec(event)?`\n   120→   - module_77's `OutboxRow::from_envelope` uses `serde_json::to_value(&envelope.event)?`\n   121→   - module_15 serializes via `serde_json::to_value(&env.event)?` in EventStore\n   122→\n   123→2. **Error Handling**: Each module defines its own error types:\n   124→   - module_15: `ServiceError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   125→   - module_48: `PublishError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   126→   - module_77: `OutboxError` enum with `Serialization` variant\n   127→\n   128→3. **Connection/Configuration Management**: Each module manages its own broker connection:\n   129→   - module_48: `NatsPublisher::connect()` with URL, JWT, prefix\n   130→   - module_77: Uses environment variables via lazy static\n   131→   - module_15: In-memory broadcast channel\n   132→\n   133→4. **Publisher Trait Definition**: Both module_48 and module_77 define their own `EventPublisher` trait with similar signatures but incompatible types.\n   134→\n   135→### Architectural Issues\n   136→\n   137→- **Code Duplication**: Serialization, error handling, and publishing logic repeated across modules\n   138→- **Inconsistent Interfaces**: Different modules use different trait signatures and error types\n   139→- **Tight Coupling**: Each module handles its own connection management\n   140→- **Maintenance Burden**: Bug fixes or improvements need to be applied in multiple places\n   141→\n   142→## Implementation\n   143→\n   144→### 1. Badge awarding micro-service with `Dispatcher` struct for event publishing via broadcast channel\n- /app/project/src/module_48.txt - Outbox → Broker integration with `EventPublisher` trait and `NatsPublisher` implementation\n- /app/project/src/module_77.txt - Outbox & Domain-Event infrastructure with `EventPublisher` trait and `StdoutPublisher` mock\n- /app/project/src/utils.txt - Utility helpers module that needs `event_bus` submodule with mock functions\n- /app/project/src/event_publisher.txt - NEW FILE: Centralized event publishing service (to be created)\n\n## Code Evidence\n\n### Module 15 - Current Event Publishing Logic\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_15.txt\n// Lines: 111-136\n\n/// Central event dispatcher.\n/// Internally uses a Tokio broadcast channel so any number of\n/// in-process consumers can receive events concurrently.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct Dispatcher {\n    sender: broadcast::Sender<EventEnvelope>,\n}\n\nimpl Dispatcher {\n    /// Create a new dispatcher with a configurable ring buffer size.\n    pub fn new(channel_size: usize) -> Self {\n        let (sender, _rx) = broadcast::channel(channel_size);\n        Self { sender }\n    }\n\n    /// Publish an envelope to all subscribers.\n    pub async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) {\n        let _ = self.sender.send(envelope);\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to a continuous stream of events.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<EventEnvelope> {\n        self.sender.subscribe()\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Module 48 - Current Event Publishing Logic\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_48.txt\n// Lines: 304-351\n\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher: Send + Sync {\n    async fn publish(&self, event: &EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), PublishError>;\n}\n\npub struct NatsPublisher {\n    conn: Connection,\n    subject_prefix: String,\n}\n\nimpl NatsPublisher {\n    pub async fn connect(\n        nats_url: &str,\n        jwt: Option<String>,\n        prefix: impl Into<String>,\n    ) -> Result<Self, PublishError> {\n        // ... connection logic\n    }\n\n    fn subject_for(&self, event_type: &str) -> String {\n        format!(\"{}.{}\", self.subject_prefix, event_type)\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EventPublisher for NatsPublisher {\n    #[instrument(skip(self, event))]\n    async fn publish(&self, event: &EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), PublishError> {\n        let subject = self.subject_for(&event.event_type);\n        let data = serde_json::to_vec(event)?;  // Serialization here\n        self.conn.publish(subject, data).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Module 77 - Current Event Publishing Logic\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_77.txt\n// Lines: 191-208\n\n/// Contract for anything that can publish events from the outbox to an external\n/// broker (NATS, RabbitMQ, Kafka, …).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher {\n    async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), anyhow::Error>;\n}\n\n/// Mock publisher for unit-testing – prints events to the console.\npub struct StdoutPublisher;\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl EventPublisher for StdoutPublisher {\n    #[instrument(level = \"info\", skip(self))]\n    async fn publish(&self, envelope: EventEnvelope) -> Result<(), anyhow::Error> {\n        println!(\"🔔 Publishing event: {envelope:#?}\");\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Duplicated Code Patterns Identified\n\n1. **Event Serialization Logic**: Each module independently serializes events using `serde_json`:\n   - module_48 uses `serde_json::to_vec(event)?`\n   - module_77's `OutboxRow::from_envelope` uses `serde_json::to_value(&envelope.event)?`\n   - module_15 serializes via `serde_json::to_value(&env.event)?` in EventStore\n\n2. **Error Handling**: Each module defines its own error types:\n   - module_15: `ServiceError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_48: `PublishError` enum with `Serde` variant\n   - module_77: `OutboxError` enum with `Serialization` variant\n\n3. **Connection/Configuration Management**: Each module manages its own broker connection:\n   - module_48: `NatsPublisher::connect()` with URL, JWT, prefix\n   - module_77: Uses environment variables via lazy static\n   - module_15: In-memory broadcast channel\n\n4. **Publisher Trait Definition**: Both module_48 and module_77 define their own `EventPublisher` trait with similar signatures but incompatible types.\n\n### Architectural Issues\n\n- **Code Duplication**: Serialization, error handling, and publishing logic repeated across modules\n- **Inconsistent Interfaces**: Different modules use different trait signatures and error types\n- **Tight Coupling**: Each module handles its own connection management\n- **Maintenance Burden**: Bug fixes or improvements need to be applied in multiple places\n\n## Implementation\n\n### 1. New File: /app/project/src/event_publisher.txt\n\n```rust\n//! src/event_publisher.rs\n//!\n//! Centralized Event Publishing Service for EduPulse Live\n//! =======================================================\n//!\n//! This module provides a unified, generic event publishing service that\n//! consolidates the previously scattered event publishing logic from various\n//! modules (module_15, module_48, module_77). It eliminates code duplication\n//! related to event serialization, connection handling, and error management.\n\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\nuse crate::utils::event_bus::{get_event_bus_config, send_event_to_stream, EventBusConfig, EventStreamError};\n\n/// Errors that can occur during event publishing.\n///\n/// This enum consolidates all error cases from the previously scattered\n/// event publishing implementations across different modules.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EventPublisherError {\n    /// Failed to serialize the event payload to JSON.\n    #[error(\"Serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Failed to connect to the event bus.\n    #[error(\"Connection error: {0}\")]\n    Connection(String),\n\n    /// Failed to send the event to the stream.\n    #[error(\"Send error: {0}\")]\n    Send(String),\n\n    /// Operation timed out.\n    #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n    Timeout(u64),\n}\n\nimpl From<EventStreamError> for EventPublisherError {\n    fn from(err: EventStreamError) -> Self {\n        match err {\n            EventStreamError::ConnectionFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Connection(msg),\n            EventStreamError::SendFailed(msg) => EventPublisherError::Send(msg),\n            EventStreamError::Timeout(ms) => EventPublisherError::Timeout(ms),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Centralized event publisher for the EduPulse Live platform.\n///\n/// This struct manages the event bus configuration and provides a single,\n/// generic method for publishing events of any serializable type.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EventPublisher {\n    config: EventBusConfig,\n}\n\nimpl EventPublisher {\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` instance.\n    ///\n    /// Retrieves configuration from the `get_event_bus_config()` function\n    /// in `crate::utils::event_bus`.\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let config = get_event_bus_config();\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a new `EventPublisher` with a custom configuration.\n    pub fn with_config(config: EventBusConfig) -> Self {\n        Self { config }\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This method handles:\n    /// - Serialization of the payload to JSON\n    /// - Sending the serialized event to the event stream\n    /// - Error handling and propagation\n    ///\n    /// # Type Parameters\n    ///\n    /// * `T` - The event payload type. Must implement `Serialize` and `Sync`.\n    pub async fn publish<T: Serialize + Sync>(&self, payload: &T) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        // Serialize the payload to JSON\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n\n        // Determine the topic based on the config\n        let topic = format!(\"{}.events\", self.config.topic_prefix);\n\n        // Send the event to the stream\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Publishes an event payload to a specific topic on the event stream.\n    pub async fn publish_to_topic<T: Serialize + Sync>(\n        &self,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &T,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventPublisherError> {\n        let json_payload = serde_json::to_string(payload)?;\n        let full_topic = format!(\"{}.{}\", self.config.topic_prefix, topic);\n        send_event_to_stream(&self.config, &full_topic, &json_payload).await?;\n\n        tracing::debug!(\n            topic = %full_topic,\n            payload_size = json_payload.len(),\n            \"Event published successfully\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a reference to the current configuration.\n    pub fn config(&self) -> &EventBusConfig {\n        &self.config\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Default for EventPublisher {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self::new()\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::Serialize;\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\n    struct TestEvent {\n        id: u64,\n        message: String,\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_event() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 1,\n            message: \"test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish(&event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_publish_to_custom_topic() {\n        let publisher = EventPublisher::new();\n        let event = TestEvent {\n            id: 2,\n            message: \"custom topic test\".to_string(),\n        };\n        let result = publisher.publish_to_topic(\"custom.topic\", &event).await;\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 2. Modifications to /app/project/src/utils.txt\n\nAdd a new `event_bus` module before the `telemetry` module:\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/utils.txt\n// Add after line 335 (after the event module closing brace)\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n * Module: event_bus\n * --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\npub mod event_bus {\n    //! Mock event bus infrastructure used by the centralized `EventPublisher` service.\n    //! In production these would connect to a real message broker like NATS or Kafka.\n\n    use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n    /// Configuration for connecting to the event bus.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    pub struct EventBusConfig {\n        pub broker_url: String,\n        pub topic_prefix: String,\n        pub max_retries: u32,\n        pub timeout_ms: u64,\n    }\n\n    impl Default for EventBusConfig {\n        fn default() -> Self {\n            Self {\n                broker_url: \"nats://localhost:4222\".to_string(),\n                topic_prefix: \"edupulse\".to_string(),\n                max_retries: 3,\n                timeout_ms: 5000,\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Retrieves the event bus configuration from environment or defaults.\n    pub fn get_event_bus_config() -> EventBusConfig {\n        EventBusConfig {\n            broker_url: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_URL\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"nats://localhost:4222\".to_string()),\n            topic_prefix: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_TOPIC_PREFIX\")\n                .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"edupulse\".to_string()),\n            max_retries: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_MAX_RETRIES\")\n                .ok()\n                .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())\n                .unwrap_or(3),\n            timeout_ms: std::env::var(\"EVENT_BUS_TIMEOUT_MS\")\n                .ok()\n                .and_then(|v| v.parse().ok())\n                .unwrap_or(5000),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Error type for event stream operations.\n    #[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error)]\n    pub enum EventStreamError {\n        #[error(\"Connection failed: {0}\")]\n        ConnectionFailed(String),\n        #[error(\"Send failed: {0}\")]\n        SendFailed(String),\n        #[error(\"Timeout after {0}ms\")]\n        Timeout(u64),\n    }\n\n    /// Sends an event payload (as a JSON string) to the event stream.\n    ///\n    /// This is a mock implementation that simulates sending events to a broker.\n    pub async fn send_event_to_stream(\n        config: &EventBusConfig,\n        topic: &str,\n        payload: &str,\n    ) -> Result<(), EventStreamError> {\n        tracing::debug!(\n            broker_url = %config.broker_url,\n            topic = %topic,\n            payload_len = payload.len(),\n            \"Sending event to stream\"\n        );\n\n        // Simulate network latency\n        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(1)).await;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### 3. The centralized `EventPublisher` should be used as a shared service instance (via `Arc<EventPublisher>`) when multiple components need to publish events\n  2.
